# Class 5 ch 10 Time — build notes (started 2026-11-16)

Plan indiannica_eng_maths5_ch10_v3 v3, 9 tools, runtime pinned **r66** (= head, silent). Full mode, all 9 at once (no pilot).

## Owner decisions (2026-11-16, step 2)
- Gemini visual pass (4b) on every tool, after the maths review ("use gemini for visuals").
- 24-hour time: FOUR digits in ONE box, "____ hours", judged as the typed string with leading zeros ("0040" right,
  "40" a miss; the hint names the zeros). Plain item + the tool's own judge/answerForms — NOT `parts` (numeric, misses a leading zero).
- 12-hour time: `parts` h | min + a big a.m. / p.m. card on the working area; judged once both given; correction names the wrong half.
- Date across a month end: type the day, tap the month card (this month / next month); month never typed as a number.
- Durations: unit-label boxes (h | min | s, days | h); seconds with hundredths on the point key.
- Clock faces are drawn round (PATTERNS §17), hands draggable where the brief turns them.

## Runtime-gap list (logged, not frozen; one freeze before the packs, one at step 6)
- (C19, 2026-11-16) `pieces()` cuts a bubble line at `[.!?]\s+`, so "a.m. " / "p.m. " in the MIDDLE of a sentence makes two pieces
  (`one-page` FAIL; a closing "... a.m. or p.m." lost its end in `one-line-bubble`). Worked round in the tool: every bubble line ends
  on a.m./p.m. or has a comma/? right after it. Every Time-chapter builder will hit it; a runtime fix would treat a.m./p.m. as abbreviations.
